  result and the run directory is absent here.
- Long-window falsification: `20260502_232935` / `20260503_145322`, XAUUSD M5 2019-2025 Model 1, 492 trades, PF `0.9520`, net `-$127.11`, DD `6.2248%`.
- Main loss pocket: `SIDEWAY_WIDE`; S1 sweep/reclaim in sideway-wide loses heavily and is not a production edge.
- Generic sideway rotation, compression breakout, micro breakout, and post-impulse retest probes are rejected from current fields.
- XAU long-bias gate is cleaner but too sparse and loses to matched control on net/cadence.
- XAU S1 M15 ATR impulse gate V1 is also too sparse: it improves PF only by deleting most S1 trades.
- XAU S1 M5 Dragon/Trend compression gate V1 is worse: it keeps most trades but removes the small bucket of high-value impulse wins.
- XAU S1 trigger micro-structure probe V1 also failed: body ratio, micro clearance, and Dragon congestion proxy do not separate impulse winners from loss/flat trades in the current sidecars.
- XAU S1 H1 structural sweep probe V1 found partial H1 sweep-depth separation, but not enough loss removal at acceptable sample/cadence. Do not convert it into a hard filter without a new independent thesis.
- EUR Asian-range manipulation has enough raw cadence, but the V1 reversal model is too weak after costs and too concentrated by year. The counter-EMA89 finding is a new post-hoc idea only.
- EUR counter-EMA high-sweep / H4 extension V1 is too sparse and not explained by H4 extension. High PF on 14 holdout trades is not evidence for a backbone.
- GBP London Dragon value-drift to EMA89 is not viable as defined: target runway is too small for cost, and PVSRA drive does not separate outcomes.
